images","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-13T08:19:48+00:00","category_id":"multimodal_gen","cover_url":"/covers/2605_13155.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文讨论多 reward 对齐中的 reward hacking：在文本生成图像模型的偏好优化里，不同 prompt 对应的图像域有不同 reward 上界，但现有方法常用统一全局目标或加权求和目标，容易让部分样本沿着 reward model 的漏洞优化，分数升高而人类感知质量下降。","importance":"这篇论文对预训练本身不是直接贡献，但对 post-train 与训练信号质量有参考价值。它把 reward hacking 的原因从“reward model 不够好”进一步拆到“不同样本的可达 reward 边界不同”。这个视角也适用于 LLM 的 RLHF、RLAIF 和多目标偏好优化：如果不同任务、语言、难度区间的 reward 分布和上界不同，用同一个全局 margin、baseline 或目标值，会把一部分样本推向不可靠区域。贡献主要在目标构造和评估指标，而不是模型架构。","technical_approach":"作者提出 Pareto Frontier-Guided Optimal Transport，简称 PG-OT。核心做法不是把多个 reward 线性加权成一个标量后统一最大化，而是为每个 prompt 构造 prompt-specific Pareto frontier。生成样本 batch 被视为源分布，Pareto frontier 被视为目标分布，然后用 optimal transport 将样本分布映射到对应 frontier。这样做的动机是尊重不同 prompt 的 reward 上界差异，避免把低上界样本硬推向不可达的全局目标。方法还区分强弱 reward model：对较可靠的 reward model 使用 online strategy，在训练中动态收集并更新 Pareto frontier；对较弱、容易坍缩的 reward model 使用 offline strategy，先预生成样本并提取 frontier，再作为更稳定的优化目标。评价上，作者提出 Joint Domination Rate 和 Joint Collapse Rate。JDR 衡量样本是否在所有 reward 上同时优于 baseline，JCR 衡量是否在所有 reward 上同时退化，比只看 reward 均值更能反映多目标优化是否真的协同。","experiments":"论文报告 PG-OT 在多 reward 文生图偏好优化上优于强 baseline。关键结果包括：JDR2 达到 47.98%，提升约 11 个百分点；JDR4 达到 17.10%，提升约 3.4 个百分点；JCR4 下降 0.2%，说明在四个 reward 同时退化的样本比例上有所抑制。人工评测中，方法获得接近 80% 的 win rate。实验结论主要支持两点：第一，均值 reward 提升不足以说明对齐质量提升；第二，prompt-specific Pareto frontier 能减少多 reward 优化里的全局目标错配。需要注意的是，全文片段没有展示完整 reward model 组合、训练超参和人评协议，复现时这些细节会决定结论强度。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 30 分钟。优先看 Mechanisms Behind Reward Hacking，理解作者如何把 heterogeneous reward bounds 和 unified global target 联系起来。然后看 PG-OT 方法部分，重点是 prompt-specific Pareto frontier 的构造、OT loss 如何进入训练、online/offline 两种策略的适用条件。实验部分重点看 JDR/JCR 与传统均值 reward 指标是否出现分歧。若研究 LLM RL，可以把文生图细节放在次要位置，重点思考它对多任务 reward normalization、per-prompt baseline、难度自适应目标和 reward model 可靠性分层的启发。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies reward hacking in multi-reward preference optimization for text-to-image models. Different prompts induce image domains with different attainable reward ranges, but common methods optimize toward a shared global target or a weighted sum of rewards. This mismatch can increase reward scores while degrading human-perceived quality.","importance":"The work is more relevant to post-training and reward-signal design than to pretraining. Its useful contribution is the diagnosis that reward hacking can be caused by heterogeneous reward upper bounds, not only by weak reward models. The same issue can appear in LLM RLHF or multi-objective alignment: tasks with different difficulty, languages, or domains may not share a single reachable reward target. A global margin, baseline, or scalarized objective can push some samples into regions where the reward model is unreliable.","technical_approach":"$25","experiments":"The reported results show consistent gains in multi-reward text-to-image alignment. PG-OT reaches 47.98% JDR2, about 11 percentage points higher than the compared baseline, and 17.10% JDR4, about 3.4 points higher. JCR4 decreases by 0.2%, indicating a reduction in samples that collapse across all four rewards. Human evaluation reports a win rate close to 80%. The main empirical message is that average reward scores are not sufficient for judging multi-reward alignment, and that prompt-specific Pareto targets can reduce global-target mismatch. Reproduction would require checking the exact reward model set, training recipe, frontier construction details, and human evaluation protocol.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 30 minutes. explicitly say a claim is false, models often behave as if the claim is true. The issue is not ordinary reading comprehension. When the same negated documents are provided in context, the models largely reject the claim. The failure appears during parameter updating, where repeated exposure to the claim dominates the attached epistemic qualifier.","importance":"This is directly relevant to pretraining, continual pretraining, and SFT data design. Training corpora contain debunks, fictional stories, counterexamples, safety warnings, and descriptions of behaviors that should not be imitated. Standard next-token training does not ensure that a qualifier such as false, fictional, unlikely, or prohibited is stored together with the content it qualifies. The result is a data-quality hazard: negated or disendorsed content can still be implanted as belief or behavior. The paper also separates in-context understanding from finetuning generalization. A model can process negation correctly in the prompt while still internalizing the negated claim during training.","technical_approach":"$26","experiments":"$27","reading_guide":"Read closely. Section 4 is important because it shows that the issue is not limited to the word “not”; it also affects fiction labels, probability qualifiers, and behavioral annotations. Section 5 is the most useful part for training-dynamics questions, especially the soft-constraint result and the instability under continued finetuning. The main technical question to examine is why local negation works while document-level and neighboring-sentence negation largely fail. Plausible mechanisms include locality in token-level loss, how event relations are compressed into parameters, and how models bind claims to epistemic status during finetuning.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"必须复查内部数据清洗策略：这篇把“辟谣语料可安全训练”的 prior 打穿了，局部否定才是关键变量。","en":"This should trigger an internal data-cleaning audit: it breaks the prior that debunking text is safe for finetuning, with local negation as the key variable."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Harry Mayne","Lev McKinney","Jan Dubiński","Adam Karvonen","James Chua","Owain Evans"],"notable_authors":["Owain Evans"],"tags":["finetuning","negation","belief-updating","data-quality","continual-pretraining"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13829","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.13829","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.12519","title":"Correct Answers from Sound Reasoning: Verifiable Process Supervision for Language Models","abstract":"Training language models to produce both correct answers and sound reasoning remains an open challenge. outcome-only RL 在推理训练中的一个具体问题：模型最终答案变准了，但显式推理过程可能变差，表现为中间判断错误、遗漏或前后不一致。作者希望在可验证领域里，同时优化最终答案和推理过程质量。","importance":"这篇论文属于 post-train，但和预训练研究有接口价值。它说明可验证奖励只看最终答案时，训练信号会鼓励捷径，而不一定鼓励可复用的中间推理状态。对 RL pretrain、reasoning-oriented midtrain、以及可验证数据构造都有启发：如果任务天然可分解，过程级 verifier 可以提供比最终答案更密的监督信号；但前提是中间声明能被稳定抽取并确定性验证。","technical_approach":"方法叫 Verifiable Process Supervision，简称 VPS。流程分三步。第一步，用合成推理轨迹做 SFT，让模型输出固定结构的 reasoning trace。结构化格式不是为了可读性，而是为了后续能用简单规则抽取中间 claims，避免依赖 LLM judge 或复杂语义解析。第二步，构造 reasoning verifier，把抽取出的中间声明和领域中的 ground-truth 信号或规则约束比较，得到多个 subtask reward。第三步，在 RL 训练中把最终答案奖励和过程奖励合并。作者还加入 adaptive reward weighting：对当前错误更大的子任务赋予更高权重，相当于让训练动态地偏向模型薄弱的 reasoning subskill。和普通 RLVR 相比，VPS 不只奖励 answer correctness；和 LLM-as-judge 过程打分相比，它更便宜，也更可控，但适用范围依赖可验证环境。","experiments":"实验选择国际象棋作为测试床，因为棋局有确定规则、引擎参考信号，并且 move analysis 可以拆成候选着法、局面评估等可解释子任务。论文报告在四个开源 reasoning models 上，accuracy-only GRPO 能提升最终预测准确率，但会降低推理质量，例如 win-rate error 增大、内部一致性下降。VPS 在保留 top-1 accuracy 收益的同时，提高 Elo rating 和 coverage，并显著降低 win-rate error，使 consistency 接近饱和。adaptive weighting 相比固定聚合更好，尤其改善语言模型本来较难的子任务。在相同准确率下，三个独立 LLM judges 更偏好过程监督模型的推理轨迹。给出的正文片段没有包含 Table 1 的具体数值，因此不应推断精确提升幅度。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 30 分钟。重点看 Adaptive verifiable process supervision 这一节，确认中间 claims 如何定义、如何抽取、每个 subtask reward 如何计算，以及 adaptive weighting 的公式。实验部分重点看 accuracy-only GRPO 与 VPS 的对照表、win-rate error / consistency / Elo 的关系，以及预算长度变化下是否出现 shortcut strategy。若关注可扩展性，需要特别看 chess verifier 的领域依赖程度，因为这是方法能否迁移到数学、代码或工具使用任务的关键限制。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a failure mode of outcome-only RL for reasoning models. Optimizing final-answer correctness can improve task accuracy while degrading the exposed reasoning trace: intermediate claims become less accurate, less complete, or internally inconsistent. The goal is to train models that produce both correct answers and verifiable reasoning steps.","importance":"This is a post-training paper, but it is relevant to pretraining-facing research on reasoning signals. It shows that final-answer verifiability alone can create shortcut incentives. When a task can be decomposed into checkable intermediate claims, process-level rewards provide denser and better aligned supervision. The limitation is also clear: the approach depends on domains where intermediate statements can be extracted reliably and checked deterministically.","technical_approach":"The proposed framework is Verifiable Process Supervision, or VPS. It first uses SFT on synthetic traces to impose a structured reasoning format. This structure is functional: it allows claims to be extracted with syntactic rules rather than LLM judging or open-ended semantic parsing. A deterministic verifier then checks these claims against domain rules and ground-truth signals, producing multiple subtask rewards. These rewards are aggregated into a reasoning reward and combined with the standard final-answer reward during RL. The paper also introduces adaptive reward weighting, which gives more weight to subtasks where the model still has larger errors. Compared with standard RLVR, VPS supervises both the conclusion and the reasoning path. Compared with LLM-judge process supervision, it is cheaper and less noisy, but less general.","experiments":"Chess is used as the controlled environment because it has exact rules, engine-derived reference signals, and naturally decomposable reasoning components such as candidate move selection and position evaluation. Across four open reasoning models, accuracy-only GRPO improves prediction accuracy but harms reasoning quality, increasing win-rate error and reducing internal consistency. VPS preserves the accuracy gains while improving Elo rating and coverage, lowering win-rate error, and pushing consistency close to saturation. Adaptive weighting improves over fixed reward aggregation, especially on subtasks that are harder for language models. At matched accuracy, three independent LLM judges prefer the traces produced by process-supervised models. The provided text does not include the numeric entries from Table 1, so exact effect sizes cannot be verified here.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 30 minutes. Researchers have explored many ways to generate top quality samples. Some works rely on rejection sampling: generating lots of synthetic samples and filtering out low-quality samples. m","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-13T08:15:48+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_13149.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"合成数据生成里缺少“可量化、面向学生模型”的数据效用度量，导致常见的 generate-then-filter 或基于大模型诊断弱点的流程要么算力浪费、要么解释性弱，且很难证明某条合成样本对下游学习到底有没有增益。论文要解决的是：能否用简单、可解释、可计算的“数据效用”信号，直接驱动生成器产出对学生模型最有训练价值的数据，而不是先海量生成再筛。","importance":"把主动学习里的 acquisition function 迁移成“合成数据生成的训练信号”，并用 critic-free 的 GRPO acquisition reward，把该 reward 作为 GRPO 的回报更新生成器。\n\n奖励设计来自五类 acquisition function，并针对 LM 做了可计算化：\n1）Confidence（不确定性/边际采样）：用学生在每个位置 top-1 与 top-2 token 概率差的序列平均作为置信度，奖励取其倒数，鼓励生成“学生最拿不准但仍可学习”的样本。\n2）Proximity（贴近任务分布）：用语义相似度约束生成样本与任务训练分布的距离，避免不确定性奖励把生成推向离题/噪声区域。\n3）Gradient Magnitude（影响函数/梯度范数风格）：用学生在该样本上的梯度强度近似“这条样本会推动参数更新多少”，鼓励高训练信号样本。\n4）Diversity（覆盖性）：鼓励批内/组内样本多样，降低模式坍塌与重复。\n5）Answer Variance（答案方差/分歧）：用学生对同一问题的多次采样或分布分歧度量不确定性与歧义性，偏向能暴露推理薄弱点的样本。\n\n训练细节上，论文强调用少量（文中提到 As a result, propagating delayed outcomes back to individual decision steps without relying on costly auxiliary value models remains an open problem.","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-13T09:10:03+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_13217.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"多轮 agent RL 里奖励通常是回合末端的稀疏信号，但优化却在 token/动作层面进行，导致信用分配（credit assignment）难：中间哪一步做对/做错很难被定位。现有办法要么引入 critic/PRM 等额外模型增加复杂度与误差，要么用 trajectory-level 的 grouped 更新但方差大、监督传播弱。论文要解决的是：在不训练 critic 的前提下，能否做“按环境 grouped policy optimization 从单轮推理扩展到多轮交互，强调以 environment step 为单位做优势估计与归一化，对后续把 RL 信号更细粒度地注入到持续学习/数据生成闭环有参考意义。","technical_approach":"GAGPO 的核心是“非参数 grouped value proxy + TD/GAE 式递推”，用采样 rollout 组构造每个 step 的价值近似，从而计算 step-aligned advantage，而不训练一个参数化 critic。\n\n做法要点：\n- 交互单位从 token 提升到 environment step（一次 action 输出对应一步），优势与更新在 step 级对齐，减少 token 级噪声。\n- 对同一状态/step 采样一组 rollouts（group），用组内统计构造 value proxy（可理解为用同组样本的回报分布做 bootstrap），再用 TD/GAE 风格把末端稀疏回报向前递推，得到每一步的 advantage。\n- 仍保持 GRPO/RLOO 一类的组内归一化与相对更新形式，以稳定训练并控制方差。\n\n相对 GRPO：GRPO 更像 sequence/trajectory 级相对回报广播；GAGPO 引入时间递推，使早期 step 也能获得更定位的学习信号。相对 PPO：避免训练 critic，减少 value 估计误差与额外训练开销。","experiments":"在 ALFWorld 与 WebShop 上，用 Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct 和 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 做对比，GAGPO 稳定优于 prompting 基线与多种 RL 长度/奖励稀疏度的敏感性。\n预计阅读时间：30–40 分钟理解方法与结论；想复现需要额外 1 小时对照实现细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Multi-turn agent RL for LLMs suffers from sparse, delayed episode-level rewards while optimization happens at token/action granularity, making temporal credit assignment hard. Existing solutions either add critics/process reward models (extra complexity and estimation error) or stay critic-free but rely on trajectory-level grouped updates with weak reward propagation and high variance. The paper targets a critic-free method that still performs step-aligned, temporally propagated credit assignment.","importance":"Primarily a post-training/agent RL contribution, but relevant to the pretrain–posttrain interface because it shows how to obtain TD/GAE-like temporal advantages without training a critic. It lowers engineering overhead for multi-turn RL and provides a cleaner way to inject temporally localized learning signals, which can matter when RL is used in continual training loops or data-generation-driven training.","technical_approach":"GAGPO (Generalized Advantage Grouped Policy Optimization) builds a non-parametric grouped value proxy from rollout groups and uses TD/GAE-style recursion to compute step-aligned advantages—without a learned critic.\n\nKey design choices:\n- Credit assignment is aligned to environment steps (each step corresponds to an agent action), not tokens, reducing noise.\n- For each step, a group of sampled rollouts provides statistics to form a value proxy; then TD/GAE-style bootstrapping propagates terminal outcomes backward through time.\n- Group-wise advantage normalization (in the spirit of GRPO/RLOO) stabilizes updates and reduces variance.\n\nCompared to GRPO: instead of broadcasting a trajectory-level signal, GAGPO propagates supervision temporally to localize which steps mattered. Compared to PPO: it avoids training a critic and the associated value estimation instability.","experiments":"Evaluated on ALFWorld and WebShop with Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct. While such test-time scaling can be effective, it cannot correct model misconceptions or adapt the model to the specific structure of an individual query. Test-time optimization addresses this limitation by enabling parameter updates during inference, but existing approaches either rely on external data or optimize generic self-super","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-13T11:27:40+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_13369.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是单个查询级别的 test-time adaptation：模型在推理时不只增加采样或搜索计算，而是根据当前 query 生成局部训练信号，并临时更新参数来改善回答。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接贡献有限，但对 test-time training 和 post-train / inference 闭环有启发。它把“查询本身包含局部分布信息”作为训练信号来源，试图在不依赖外部检索库的情况下做 per-query 参数适配。这个方向值得关注的原因是，它把推理时算力从纯搜索转向临时学习，可能影响未来模型在预训练阶段是否需要显式支持快速局部适应。","technical_approach":"QueST 的流程很直接。给定 query q，先用模型自身或辅助生成器生成一批与 q 结构相近的 problem–solution pairs。这些样本被视为 query 所诱导的局部分布 D(q) 的近似。然后在推理时用这些样本做轻量 LoRA 更新，得到临时参数，再用适配后的模型回答原始 query。它和 TTT-NN 的区别是不用外部相似题检索库；和 TLM 这类最小化输入 perplexity 的方法不同，它显式生成带解答的 query-conditioned supervision，因此训练目标更贴近当前问题所需的推理结构。它和 self-evolving 方法也不同，后者通常做全局数据生成和后续训练，而 QueST 是单 query 条件下的局部更新。","experiments":"作者报告在 7 个数学推理 benchmark 上评测，QueST 相对对应 base model 平均提升 6.44 个百分点，并在 GPQA-Diamond 上展示了数学之外的科学推理泛化。给出的全文片段没有包含完整表格、模型规模、生成样本数量、LoRA rank、更新步数、额外推理成本和消融数字，因此无法核实提升主要来自 query-conditioned 生成、LoRA 更新，还是额外采样计算。阅读时需要特别检查这些控制实验。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 20–30 分钟，重点看方法部分的 D(q) 构造、test-time LoRA 更新设置，以及实验中的 cost-normalized 对比。最需要看的消融是：只生成不更新、随机相似题更新、输入 perplexity 更新、不同生成样本数量、不同更新步数、错误伪标签对最终答案的影响。如果没有严格控制推理预算，6.44 个百分点的提升不能直接理解为参数适配本身的收益。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses per-query test-time adaptation for LLMs. Instead of only spending more inference compute through sampling or search, it builds supervision from the input query itself and performs a temporary parameter update before producing the final answer.","importance":"The contribution is more relevant to test-time training and the pretrain–posttrain–inference interface than to base pretraining directly. The useful idea is that an input query may define a local problem distribution, and that generated samples from this local distribution can serve as adaptation data. If this line of work holds up under careful compute controls, it may influence how future pretrained models are designed to support fast local adaptation at inference time.","technical_approach":"QueST starts from a query q and generates auxiliary problem–solution pairs conditioned on q, using the base model itself in the described implementation. These generated examples are treated as an empirical approximation to a local distribution D(q) that shares concepts, assumptions, and reasoning patterns with the original query. The model then performs a lightweight LoRA update at test time on these examples, and the adapted parameters are used to answer q. Compared with TTT-NN, it does not require an external retrieval database of similar problems. Compared with objectives such as minimizing the perplexity of the input, it uses explicit query-conditioned problem–solution supervision. Compared with self-evolving methods, it performs localized per-query adaptation rather than global data generation and later training.","experiments":"The authors report results on seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks, with an average gain of 6.44 percentage points over the corresponding base models. They also evaluate on GPQA-Diamond to test transfer beyond mathematics into scientific reasoning. The provided full-text excerpt does not include the complete experimental tables, model sizes, number of generated examples, LoRA rank, update steps, inference cost, or ablation numbers. Those details are needed to separate the benefit of query-conditioned generation from the benefit of additional sampling compute and from the LoRA update itself.","reading_guide":"A practical skim should take 20–30 minutes. Focus on how D(q) is generated, how the LoRA update is configured, and whether the comparisons are normalized for test-time compute. The most important ablations are generation without parameter updates, random or weakly related generated problems, input-perplexity test-time training, different numbers of generated examples, different update steps, and robustness to wrong pseudo-solutions. Without those controls, the reported 6.44-point gain should be read as a system-level improvement rather than clean evidence for per-query parameter adaptation.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇不改 pretrain prior，但值得看它如何把“query 自带监督”落成训练目标；先读方法和 ablation，若增益只出现在窄任务上，就当 test-time tuning 的弱信号。","en":"It likely won’t change your pretraining prior, but it is worth reading for how it turns the query itself into a training signal; read the method and ablations first, and treat it as weak evidence if gains stay narrow."},"metadata":{"institutions":["KAIST","Graduate School of Green Growth and Sustainability","Graduate School of Green Growth and Sustainability, KAIST"],"authors":["Chaehee Song","Minseok Seo","Yeeun Seong","Doyi Kim","Changick Kim"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["test-time-training","self-training","query-conditioned","parameter-efficient-tuning","reasoning"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13369","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.13369","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.13301","title":"Achieving Gold-Medal-Level Olympiad Reasoning via Simple and Unified Scaling","abstract":"Recent progress in reasoning models has substantially advanced long-horizon mathematical and scientific problem solving, with several systems now reaching gold-medal-level performance on International Mathematical Olympiad (IMO) and International Physics Olympiad (IPhO) problems. No current approach can evaluate the actual optimization contribution of individual reasoning steps.","importance":"Provides an optimization-focused new perspective for reasoning post-training data curation, eliminates the need for external reward models or step-level human annotations, significantly reduces post-training compute overhead, and selected data subsets are transferable across different model backbones.","technical_approach":"Frames reasoning traces as sequences of optimization events. Existing objectives typically weight the teacher's token-level signal uniformly across a chain-of-thought sequence, despite substantial variation in the entropy of the teacher's predictive distribution. We propose EGRSD (Entropy-Guided Reinforced Self-Distillation), which unifies token-level updates through three","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-13T09:38:20+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_13255.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在 on-policy 自蒸馏（Self-Distillation）训练推理模型时，教师模型提供的 token 级别监督信号可靠性存在巨大差异（有些是确定性计算，有些是高熵的分支点），均匀赋予权重会导致模型过度拟合高方差的监督信号。","importance":"为 on-policy 自蒸馏引入了“教师预测熵”作为第三种监督信号（与奖励方向和教师-学生似然比并列），提供了一种更精细、更稳定的 token 级别权重分配机制，有助于在保持推理准确率的同时减少冗余计算。","technical_approach":"提出了 EGRSD（Entropy-Guided Reinforced Self-Distillation）。核心机制是计算特权教师（privileged teacher）在每个 token 处的预测熵，并用其构建一个置信度门控（confidence gate）。低熵的确定性 token 获得近乎全额的权重，而高熵的分支 token 权重被衰减，但保留一个非零下界以避免丢失有效的分支多样性。进一步提出了 CL-EGRSD，引入因果前瞻（causal-lookahead）窗口，以区分持续的高熵片段和短暂的策略转换点（pivots），从而保留后者带来的关键转换信号。","experiments":"在 Qwen3-4B 和 Qwen3-8B 上进行了验证。结果表明，置信度门控更新在准确率-长度权衡（accuracy-length frontier）上优于其他可训练方法。消融实验显示，适度的熵衰减能带来最稳定的性能；前瞻机制（lookahead）在更大模型（8B）上效果更明显，因为大模型更容易利用策略转换点。","reading_guide":"建议重点阅读 Section 4.2 中 EGRSD 的置信度门控公式设计，以及 CL-EGRSD 如何通过前瞻窗口处理瞬态高熵 token。实验部分关注图 1 中的准确率-长度权衡曲线，以及消融实验中对不同熵衰减程度的分析。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In on-policy self-distillation for reasoning models, the reliability of token-level supervision from the teacher model varies significantly (e.g., deterministic computation vs. high-entropy branching points). Uniformly weighting these signals can lead to overfitting on high-variance supervision.","importance":"Introduces 'teacher predictive entropy' as a third supervision signal (alongside reward direction and teacher-student likelihood ratio) in on-policy self-distillation. This provides a more granular and stable token-level weighting mechanism, helping to reduce redundant computation while maintaining reasoning accuracy.","technical_approach":"Proposes EGRSD (Entropy-Guided Reinforced Self-Distillation). The core mechanism computes the privileged teacher's predictive entropy at each token to construct a confidence gate. Advantages are computed independently for each level and then accumulated, transforming single-reward optimization into a multi-reward formulation. This ordinal decomposition confines evaluator stochasticity to narrow boundary strata, preventing individual noisy samples from corrupting the global mean and variance used for normalization, and preserving stable learning signals across most of the reward distribution. A variance-aware weighting mechanism is also introduced to dynamically suppress weights of evaluation criteria that are either fully satisfied or currently unreachable, focusing model optimization on the active learning frontier. Reverse KL allows the student to learn on its own rollouts and therefore fits naturally into RL-style training, but when the teacher and student distributions are far apart, the teacher mostly acts as a post-hoc scorer of bad student tokens. It does not provide a clear direction toward better tokens.","importance":"The main relevance is training-signal design rather than architecture. It shows that the direction of KL and the source of sampled states can change what supervision actually means. RKL is on-policy but weakly directional. SFT is directional but off-policy. TGPO keeps student-generated states while using teacher predictions as dense token-level guidance. This is useful for reasoning distillation, early-stage RLVR for small models, and the interface between pretraining, distillation, and reinforcement learning.","technical_approach":"The paper first formulates RKL distillation as an expectation over student samples and connects it to an RL objective with an intrinsic reward given by the negative student-teacher log-ratio. The weakness is that this reward only evaluates actions already sampled by the student. If a sampled token lies in a low-probability region under the teacher, the signal mainly says “do not do this,” without indicating the better action. TGPO changes the supervision path. The student still produces on-policy rollouts. For each student-generated context, the teacher is queried for its preferred next-token behavior, and the student is trained to increase the likelihood of the teacher’s token prediction on those visited states. This resembles SFT on student states rather than teacher states. The paper also discusses whether teacher information should be injected as a reward or as a regularizing loss; the central concern is preserving dense, low-variance token supervision.","experiments":"The reported evidence supports three claims. Standard RKL becomes unstable or unhelpful when the teacher-student gap is large. TGPO remains useful in such far-distribution settings because it supplies directional token supervision on the states the student actually visits. It is also reported to be competitive with mixed-policy approaches while requiring no additional annotation and remaining compatible with RLVR pipelines. tran","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-12T20:46:05+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_12741.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体、也很棘手的 post-train 学习问题：当环境奖励极稀疏、成功轨迹几乎拿不到，但环境又能返回较丰富的执行反馈时，模型怎样从失败中稳定起步。已有 on-policy self-distillation，如 SDPO/OPSD，已经把轨迹级稀疏奖励改写成 token 级蒸馏信号，但它们通常只是把环境反馈原样拼到 teacher prompt 里，默认模型自己会从这些原始错误信息中提炼出可学习的修正信号。作者指出，真正的瓶颈不只是有没有反馈，而是反馈是否被组织成可迁移、可复用、可监督的形式。尤其在 rare-success regime 下，teacher 几乎只能依赖失败轨迹来给 student 提供监督；如果失败反馈只是未结构化日志、报错信息或 binary outcome，teacher 给出的 token 分布改进会很弱，学生也很难靠单次 rollout 自举起来。","importance":"它对预训练本身不是直接方法贡献，更接近 pretrain↔post-train 接口上的一个信号工程问题：如何把环境交互中的弱信号转成更密、更稳定的学习目标。启发点有两层。第一，self-distillation 的效果高度依赖“反馈表示”，而不是只依赖蒸馏目标形式；这和预训练里数据格式、标注结构、target design 影响训练动态是同一类问题。第二，它提示一种比纯 RL 更样本节省的 bootstrapping 路径：先把失败经验转成局部纠错知识，再用蒸馏把这些知识压回基础策略；等成功率起来后，再让 reward-based optimization 接管。这种分阶段思路，对持续学习、在线 mid-train、agentic post-train 都有参考价值。贡献量级上，它更像一个实用而清晰的训练框架改造，不是新理论范式，但在“稀疏成功 + 丰富反馈”的设置里有明确增益。","technical_approach":"$2b","experiments":"实验重点不是大规模通用 supervision。\n- 只有局部 reflection vs. reflection + global playbook：playbook 带来跨 step 的知识保留，减少每次都从头解释失败。\n- 与 reward-based 方法比较：Resd 在 sample efficiency 和 early-stage bootstrap 上更占优，但作者并没有宣称替代 RL，而是把 RL 视为成功率上升后的互补优化器。\n\n给出的全文片段没有完整表格数字、参数量和数据规模细节，因此无法可靠填入具体百分点、模型大小或 token 数。能确认的是，实验协议强调 online streaming、single-rollout setting，以及在 sparse binary reward 之外利用 rich execution feedback。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓三处。\n\n第一，看 Introduction 和 Figure 1。这里把问题界定得很清楚：不是 self-distillation 无效，而是 raw failure feedback 的表示太弱。Figure 1 应该是理解作者动机的关键图。\n\n第二，看方法部分里 teacher context 的构造流程。重点不是 f-divergence 公式本身，而是 reflection 如何生成、playbook 如何维护、它们怎样进入 teacher prompt。这里决定了方法到底是在“做更好的提示工程”，还是在“做可持续的监督信号重写”。\n\n第三，看实验中的 early-stage curves 和 ablation。优先关注 rare-success 初期、single-rollout 条件下的学习曲线，以及 raw feedback / reflection / playbook 三者的拆分对比。这些结果最能说明方法是否真的在从失败中学习，而不是只是在成功样本出现后放大收益。\n\n如果时间有限，预计 20–30 分钟可以完成泛读：读引言、方法总览、两三个核心实验图表即可。若想判断它对 continual pretrain 或 online post-train 是否有迁移价值，再补 appendix 里关于 f-divergence 选择和训练稳定性的说明。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific failure mode in online post-training: how to improve from interaction when successful trajectories are extremely rare, yet the environment provides rich execution feedback. Existing on-policy self-distillation methods such as SDPO/OPSD already replace sparse trajectory rewards with token-level supervision from a self-teacher, but they mostly treat environment feedback as a passive prompt attachment. That works poorly in rare-success regimes, because the teacher has to infer corrective supervision almost entirely from failed rollouts. If the feedback remains as raw logs, error strings, or binary outcomes, the resulting teacher distribution is only weakly improved, and the student cannot bootstrap effectively.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, but it is relevant to the pretrain–post-train interface because it studies how weak interaction signals can be converted into denser and more reusable learning targets. Two points matter. First, the effectiveness of self-distillation depends heavily on feedback representation, not just on the divergence objective. That is closely related to target design and data formatting issues in pretraining. Second, it suggests a practical bootstrapping recipe for sparse-reward settings: first turn failures into localized corrective knowledge, distill that knowledge back into the base policy, and only rely more on reward optimization once success becomes frequent enough. The contribution is best read as a clean training-framework improvement rather than a new learning paradigm, but it addresses a real bottleneck in rare-success online learning.","technical_approach":"$2c","experiments":"$2d","reading_guide":"$2e","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得看它如何把“失败反馈”变成 token 级纠错监督；但要重点审计反思生成是否泄漏答案/引入额外算力，先读方法与消融再信结论。","en":"Worth reading for the mechanism that converts failure feedback into token-level corrective supervision; scrutinize leakage/extra-compute in reflection generation—read method + ablations before trusting the gains."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Georgia Institute of Technology"],"authors":["Yuwei Zhang","Sha Li","Changlong Yu","Qin Lu","Shuowei Jin","Chengyu Dong","Haoran Liu","Ilgee Hong","Xintong Li","Zhenyu Shi","Bing Yin","Jingbo Shang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["self-distillation","reflection","continual-learning","rare-success","feedback-learning"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12741","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.12741","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.12798","title":"Emergent and Subliminal Misalignment Through the Lens of Data-Mediated Transfer","abstract":"Fine-tuning LLMs on narrow harmful datasets can induce Emergent Misalignment (EM), where models exhibit misaligned behavior far beyond the fine-tuning distribution. With long-context models, many-shot ICL can use dozens to hundreds of examples and achieve performance comparable to fine-tuning, yet current understanding of its scaling behavior is largely derived from non-reasoning tasks. We study many-shot chain-of-thought in-context learning (CoT-ICL) for reasoning and show that standard many-shot rules do not","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-13T13:30:12+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_13511.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是：many-shot ICL 在推理任务上到底是否真的“随 shot 数增加而稳定变强”，以及已有的 many-shot 经验法则能否直接迁移到 CoT-ICL。全文给出的答案是否定的。对分类等非推理任务，更多 demonstrations 往往带来较稳定的收益，且顺序影响会随 shot 增大而减弱；但在数学、叙事推理这类需要中间过程的任务上，many-shot CoT-ICL 的行为明显不同。非 reasoning-oriented 模型在 shot 增加后常出现不稳定、甚至退化；reasoning-oriented 模型才更容易从大量 CoT 示例中持续获益。作者进一步指出，问题不只在“示例是否相似”，而在示例的推理过程是否对当前模型可理解、相邻示例之间的知识过渡是否平滑。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要有两层。第一，它提醒人们不要把非推理 ICL 的 scaling 规律直接外推到推理场景。长上下文和更多示例，并不自动等于更强的 test-time learning；模型是否具备足够的推理先验，会决定它能否“吃下”这些 CoT 轨迹。第二，这篇工作把 many-shot CoT-ICL 从“表面模式匹配”重新解释为一种受模型能力约束的 in-context test-time learning，这个视角对理解 pretrain 与 post-train 的接口很有价值：如果模型在预训练或后训练阶段没有形成足够稳固的过程建模能力，那么推理 demonstrations 只是更长的噪声；反过来，若模型已有较好的过程先验，prompt 中的 CoT 才更像课程式增量学习。贡献量级不在于提出全新架构，而在于把一个常被经验化处理的问题拆成了可分析的两个原则，并给出可操作的排序方法。","technical_approach":"方法上分两部分。第一部分是系统实验框架。作者同时覆盖任务类型、模型类型和 ICL 配置三条轴：任务上既看非推理任务，也看 GSM8K、MATH、DetectiveQA 这类推理任务；模型上区分普通 instruction-tuned LLM 与显式 reasoning-oriented LLM；配置上比较不同 shot 数、是否使用 CoT、不同示例选择与排序策略。评测统一采用 open-ended generation，再做答案抽取和 exact match，避免分类任务与生成任务使用完全不同的评测协议。第二部分是解释与方法。作者提出两个原则：Ease of Understanding，即 demonstration 的推理方式要落在模型当前能力附近，否则长 CoT 只会增加理解负担；Smoothness of Knowledge Progression，即相邻 demonstrations 之间的概念跳跃不能太大，否则模型在上下文中难以形成稳定的过程迁移。基于第二点，他们提出 Curvilinear Demonstration Selection, CDS。做法不是按 query-demo 相似度独立选样，而是把 demonstrations 看成一条嵌入空间中的轨迹，排序时尽量最小化整条轨迹的“曲率”，让概念演进更平滑。这个设计与传统 similarity retrieval 的区别很明确：后者关注每个示例与测试问题像不像，CDS 关注整串示例之间是否构成可学习的教学序列。全文还强调，自生成 demonstrations 对弱模型更有效，这与 Ease of Understanding 是一致的，因为模型自己生成的推理链通常更贴近其内部表征与可执行风格。","experiments":"从全文给出的结果看，最重要的实验结论有四个。第一，many-shot scaling 是 setting-dependent 的：在非推理任务上，shot 增加通常继续带来收益；在推理任务上，收益高度依赖模型是否是 reasoning-oriented。第二，相似度检索在非推理任务上仍然有效，但在推理任务上解释力明显下降，因为题面相似不代表解题程序兼容。第三，顺序效应在 CoT-ICL 中没有像分类任务那样随着 shot 增大而被平均掉，反而会出现更大的方差，也就是 demonstrations 越多，排序越可能决定成败。第四，CDS 在推理任务上带来可见收益，文中最明确的数字是 geometry 场景下 64 demonstrations 时最高提升 5.42 个百分点。数据集方面，推理任务包括 GSM8K、MATH、DetectiveQA，非推理任务包括 SuperGLUE、TREC、BANKING77 等。评测采用 exact match，数值题会抽取最终数值或表达式。虽然摘要截断了部分数字，全文主线已经足够清楚：收益不是普遍单调的，且模型类型与示例组织方式比单纯 shot 数更关键。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议先看 Introduction 和实验设定，先把作者要反驳的旧经验法则记清楚：‘更多 shot 更稳’、‘相似度检索有效’、‘顺序影响会消失’。然后重点看结果图里三类对比：非推理任务 vs 推理任务、普通模型 vs reasoning-oriented 模型、similarity retrieval vs CDS。若时间有限，优先抓住两个点：一是 many-shot CoT 的收益依赖模型是否已经具备过程推理能力；二是 demonstration ordering 在推理场景不是边缘问题，而是核心变量。再看方法部分对 curvature 的定义和排序策略，理解作者为什么不用单点相似度而改用轨迹平滑度。预计 25 到 35 分钟可以完成有效泛读；如果要深挖，可继续看附录中的 prompt 模板、答案抽取规则，以及不同任务上的顺序方差分析。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a precise question: does many-shot ICL continue to scale in reasoning settings once demonstrations include chain-of-thought, and do the standard many-shot rules from non-reasoning tasks still hold? The answer is largely no. For classification-like tasks, adding more demonstrations often helps and order sensitivity tends to wash out at larger shot counts. For reasoning tasks such as math and narrative reasoning, many-shot CoT-ICL behaves differently. Non-reasoning-oriented models show unstable scaling and can even degrade as more CoT demonstrations are added, while reasoning-oriented models benefit much more consistently. The paper argues that the key issue is not just input similarity, but whether the reasoning traces are understandable to the model and whether adjacent demonstrations form a smooth progression.","importance":"Its value for pretraining-oriented readers is twofold. First, it cautions against transferring scaling intuitions from non-reasoning ICL to reasoning. A longer context and more demonstrations do not automatically produce stronger test-time learning; the model must already have enough process-level reasoning prior to absorb those CoT traces. Second, the paper reframes many-shot CoT-ICL as in-context test-time learning rather than surface pattern matching. That framing matters for the pretrain/post-train interface: if pretraining or post-training did not build robust process representations, long CoT prompts are mostly extra noise; if the model already has a usable reasoning prior, demonstrations can act more like a curriculum. The contribution is not a new architecture, but a useful decomposition of the problem into two analyzable principles and a practical ordering method built on them.","technical_approach":"$31","experiments":"$32","reading_guide":"For a skim, start with the introduction and setup to lock in the three assumptions the paper is challenging: more shots should scale smoothly, similarity retrieval should work, and order should matter less at larger shot counts. Then focus on the result figures comparing non-reasoning versus reasoning tasks, standard versus reasoning-oriented models, and similarity retrieval versus CDS. If time is limited, keep two takeaways in view: many-shot CoT only helps reliably when the model already has process-level reasoning competence, and demonstration ordering is a first-order issue in reasoning prompts. After that, read the method section defining curvature and the CDS ordering objective to understand why the authors move away from pointwise similarity toward trajectory smoothness. A useful skim should take about 25 to 35 minutes. Existing SAEs read residual streams, but Gated DeltaNet, Mamba-2, and RWKV-7 write to a $d_k \\times d_v$ cache through rank-1 updates $k_t v_t^\\top$ that no vector atom can replace. WriteSAE factors each decoder atom into the native write shape, exposes a closed form for the per-token logit shift, and trains un","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-12T21:32:45+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_12770.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇文章解决的是：对 Mamba-2、RWKV-7、Gated DeltaNet 这类矩阵状态/混合递归语言模型，传统 residual-stream SAE 无法直接解释模型真正写入 recurrent state 的内容，因为这些模型每个 token 写入的是 rank-1 外积形式的矩阵更新，而不是一个普通向量特征。作者要做的是构造一种与原生写入形状匹配的 SAE，使得“解释”和“干预”都发生在 cache write 这一层，而不是事后在 residual 上读影子信号。","importance":"对预训练本身的直接影响有限，它更偏机制分析与可解释性工具，而不是训练方法或数据配方。但它有两个启发点。第一，随着非 Transformer 或 hybrid recurrent LM 重新进入视野，解释工具不能默认 residual stream 是唯一合适接口，必须贴着模型的原生状态更新机制来做。第二，若未来有人研究 state-space LM 的训练动态、记忆容量或 feature superposition，这种 write-level 分解可能会成为比 residual SAE 更自然的分析入口。就今日 digest 的相关性而言，它属于可泛读的机制工作，不是预训练 recipe 级别的论文。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 WriteSAE。普通 SAE 的 decoder atom 是向量，适合重构 residual activation；但在 Gated DeltaNet 一类模型里，每个 token 对状态矩阵 S_t 的写入是 k_t v_t^T 这样的 rank-1 外积。若仍用向量 atom 去分解 vec(S_t)，虽然数学上可行，但干预时无法对应到“替换一次原生写入事件”。作者因此把 SAE decoder atom 约束成与原生写入同形的 rank-1 外积 v_i w_i^T，并在 mean-centered state 上训练 TopK SAE，损失是重构误差加 dead-feature revival 项。这样每个 atom 都可以被解释为“一个可替换的 cache-slot write primitive”。\n\n在此基础上，论文提出三类验证。第一是 cache-slot substitution：在某个 firing 上，用学到的 atom 替换原始 native write，并与 matched-norm ablation 对照，比较继续前向传播后的输出 KL，检验 atom 是否比简单删掉写入更接近原模型行为。第二是 closed-form logit shift：作者推导了一个三因子表达式，把单次写入扰动对后续 token logit 的影响分解为 gate 衰减项、read query 项和 unembedding 项。这个公式的意义在于，它把 recurrent state 干预和最终词表 logit 变化连起来，而且每一项都能从一次 forward pass 观测。第三是直接干预实验，包括 erase、install 和 generation probe，用来验证这些 atom 不只是重构工具，而是可以定向改变模型续写。\n\n论文还区分了两类 atom：register 和 bundle。前者的写入方向可以从 cache 中较清晰恢复，后者则更分散。作者用这一点讨论不同矩阵递归架构的“写入秩”差异，并把 GDN、RWKV-7、Mamba-2 按 register-bundle cosine 指标排序，试图说明哪些架构更接近可由低秩写入解释的状态更新。","experiments":"关键实验集中在 Qwen3.5-0.8B 的某一层头位点，以及 Mamba-2-370M 的迁移验证。最核心的 substitution test 在 Qwen3.5-0.8B L9 H4 上进行：在 4851 次 atom firing 中，学到的 atom 替换 native write 后，有 92.4% 的情况优于 matched-norm ablation；在 87 个 atom 的 population test 上，这个比例是 89.8%。这说明 learned atom 不是简单学到“删掉某个写入”，而是较好逼近了原始写入的功能结构。\n\nclosed-form logit shift 的拟合结果也很强。作者在 200 个 atom-by-epsilon 单元上比较解析预测与实测 logit shift，中位数 R^2 达到 0.98。若这个结果稳健，说明在 Gated DeltaNet 这类门控递归里，单次写入对后续输出的影响确实可以被一个相对低维、可解释的链式表达捕捉。\n\n干预实验方面，擦除某个 atom（文中举 F412）在其原生 firing 上会让被促进 token 的 log-prob 平均下降 0.116 nats，n=150，p=1.07e-6。单位置 predictive install 在 2000 个 atom-token-context 三元组上有 84.6% 的符号预测正确率。持续三位置 install、放大 3 倍时，在 300 个上下文上把 target-in-continuation 从 33.3% 提升到 100%，首步支持增加 1.27 nats。迁移到 Mamba-2-370M L24 H0 时，substitution 胜过 ablation 的比例为 88.08%，说明方法并非只对一个模型实例有效。\n\n论文也给了边界条件。作者明确列出若干 null result，包括 4B firing-level、Mamba-2 closed-form、top-1-matched rank-2、以及 Mamba-2 generation intervention 等未成功或未完全成立的设置。这一点很重要，说明当前 closed-form 更依赖 GDN 风格 gate，而不是已经成为所有矩阵递归模型上的通用理论。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction，确认它解决的不是一般 SAE，而是“如何对 recurrent matrix write 做 SAE”。然后看 Method 里 decoder atom 的 rank-1 约束，以及 closed-form logit shift 的三因子分解。理解这两点，基本就抓住了论文的核心。\n\n如果只花 20–30 分钟，建议看三处：一是 substitution test 的定义和主结果表，这决定了方法是否真的贴近 native write；二是 closed-form 推导后的验证图，看 R^2=0.98 是在哪些条件下成立；三是 intervention 结果，尤其是 erase/install 是否能稳定改变续写。附录里关于 Householder-modulated propagator 的推导可以按需看，不必一开始就啃完。\n\n值得留意的地方有两个。第一，作者很诚实地给出了若干失败设置，这些地方比成功案例更能说明方法边界。第二，若你关心的是预训练而不是纯解释，重点不是某个 atom 对某个 token 的控制效果，而是这种 write-level 分解是否能帮助分析 state-space LM 的记忆容量、训练阶段 feature 形成、以及不同架构的状态更新秩结构。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a limitation of standard sparse autoencoders for recurrent and state-space language models. In architectures such as Gated DeltaNet, Mamba-2, and RWKV-7, each token writes a rank-1 matrix update into a recurrent cache. Residual-stream SAEs operate downstream and cannot directly decompose or edit the native write event itself. The goal here is to build an SAE whose atoms live in the same space and shape as the model’s write primitive, so that interpretation and intervention happen at the recurrent-state write rather than on a later residual shadow.","importance":"Its direct relevance to pretraining is limited; this is primarily a mechanistic interpretability paper. Still, it has two useful implications. First, if recurrent or state-space LMs become more important again, interpretability tools cannot assume the residual stream is always the right interface. They need to match the model’s native state update. Second, for future work on memory capacity, feature superposition, or training dynamics in non-Transformer LMs, write-level decomposition may be a more natural probe than residual SAEs. So this is a skim paper for a pretraining researcher: not a recipe paper, but a potentially useful tool paper.","technical_approach":"$33","experiments":"$34","reading_guide":"$35","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"读方法和 logit-shift 推导；若还在评估 Mamba/RWKV，这比残差 SAE 更贴近真实状态写入，但规模仍偏小。","en":"Read the method and logit-shift derivation; for Mamba/RWKV-style models it is closer to the real state write than residual SAEs, though still small-scale."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Indiana University"],"authors":["Jack Young"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["sparse-autoencoder","interpretability","state-space-models","recurrent-lm","cache-editing"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12770","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.12770","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.13156","title":"Dual-Pathway Circuits of Object Hallucination in Vision-Language Models","abstract":"Vision-language models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in bridging visual perception and natural language understanding, enabling a wide range of multimodal reasoning tasks. 注入视觉信息。每个样本运行 clean pass、corrupt pass 和 patch pass。corrupt pass 在第 0 层视觉 token 上加入 3σ 高斯噪声，使视觉信息基本失效；patch pass 只恢复某一层某一组件的 clean activation。评估指标是最终 token 位置上正确答案与错误答案的 logit 差。若恢复某组件能显著提升该差值，说明该组件承载了视觉证据。作者进一步用 Welch t-test、Benjamini-Hochberg 多重校正和 Cohen’s d 筛出与正确样本、幻觉样本差异显著的组件。d>0 的组件归为 visual grounding pathway，d<0 的组件归为 hallucination pathway。论文还提出 Conditional Pathway Analysis，用来分析同一路径内组件之间的条件依赖，发现 grounding pathway 在正确样本和幻觉样本之间存在 polarity flip，这一点单组件 patching 不容易看出来。","experiments":"实验在每个模型 1000 个 POPE-adversarial 样本上做 patching，样本被分成正确回答“no”和幻觉回答“yes”两组。筛选电路时要求校正后 p<0.05 且 |Cohen’s d|>0.3。主要发现是五个结构不同的 VLM 都出现双路径组织：一条路径支持视觉证据传递，另一条路径更容易推动语言先验式的错误回答。因果验证采用 component-wise suppression，对 hallucination pathway 做定向抑制，最多可降低 76% 的对象幻觉，同时准确率代价不超过 2 个百分点。作者还比较了 matched static-direction baseline，发现逐组件缩放更有效，说明问题不只是某个固定方向上的全局偏置。迁移实验显示，该电路能迁移到 AMBER 的关系幻觉，但不能很好迁移到属性幻觉，提示对象存在和关系判断可能共享部分视觉-语言竞争机制，而属性错误涉及更细粒度的视觉表征。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 Introduction 和 Methodology，重点看 Component-wise suppression of the hallucination pathway reduces object hallucination by up to 76% with no more than a 2 percentage-point accuracy cost. Experimental results reveal systematic flaws of existing models in cross-task resource planning, providing a benchmark and improvement direction for agent inference efficiency optimization and resource scheduling capability training.","technical_approach":"The TRIAGE evaluation framework is designed as follows: 1. The input includes a task pool, point value per task, and a token budget calibrated to the tested model's own baseline cost on the task set; 2. The model is required to output a complete prospective execution plan covering task selection, execution order, and token allocation per task; 3. The plan score is calculated against an oracle plan that has full knowledge of the model's solvability and cost on each task; 4. It covers three metacognitive control functions (selection, allocation, termination) defined in cognitive psychology, and evaluates behavioral output instead of the model's self-reported confidence to avoid the unreliability of self-report signals.","experiments":"20 model architectures, including 4 standard LLMs and 16 reasoning-augmented LLMs, are evaluated across 4 task domains (competition mathematics, graduate-level science knowledge, code generation, multidisciplinary expert knowledge) with thinking mode both enabled and disabled. Key findings: 1. Metacognitive control capability transfers poorly across domains; 2. Reasoning augmentation improves single-task accuracy but not resource scheduling quality; 3. Reasoning-trained models are less likely to identify unsolvable items in a task pool; 4. Models can preliminarily select tasks to attempt but rarely formulate budget allocation plans that they themselves can strictly adhere to, with large deviations in budget execution across all budget levels.","reading_guide":"Focus on Chapter 2 for the TRIAGE framework design to understand the core logic of the evaluation paradigm and its difference from traditional single-task confidence evaluation. sequenc","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-13T15:42:26+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_13687.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","stat.ML"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文想回答一个更基础的问题：自回归语言模型的上下文长度到底买到了什么，链式推理式的中间 token 又在什么条件下能替代更长上下文。作者没有直接分析真实语言，而是构造一类可精确求解的层级语言，用树上的 broadcast process 生成叶子序列，从而把长程依赖、隐变量结构、上下文窗口和生成分布偏差放到一个可证明的框架里。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要在理论建模，而不是给出新的训练 recipe。它把 context scaling 和 reasoning benefit 从经验现象拆成可计算对象：有限上下文的模型会如何扭曲生成分布的高阶统计，哪些层级相关性需要覆盖到共同祖先才能恢复，哪些情况下额外的中间 token 可以把隐式长程依赖转成局部条件依赖。这个框架对理解长上下文、CoT、以及层级数据分布下的 next-token objective 有参考价值，但离真实 LLM 的预测性还需要谨慎外推。","technical_approach":"核心方法是用最优 k-gram 自回归过程近似一个上下文长度为 k 的 transformer。也就是假设模型在每个位置只能使用前 k 个 token，并学习该条件分布下的最优 next-token 预测。通常大 k 的 k-gram 不可分析，但在作者构造的层级语言中，k-gram 分布可以精确计算。语言由 d 叉树上的 broadcast process 产生：根节点从先验采样，内部节点通过噪声信道向下传播，最终叶子节点按固定遍历顺序组成序列。作者研究了两类实例：Ising broadcast，token 为 ±1，用来表示软的全局相关；coloring broadcast，子节点颜色必须不同于父节点，用来表示硬约束。实验中还加入层级标点，帮助有限上下文模型定位自己处在树的哪个局部结构中。推理版本的模型在生成时写出额外中间 token，相当于显式恢复部分隐层节点，使原本跨远距离的依赖变成可局部使用的信息。","experiments":"论文的关键实验是检验 k-gram ansatz 是否能预测 transformer 的生成统计。以 Ising broadcast 为例，图 1 使用 d=3、h=8、rho=0.9 的设置，叶子数为 3^8=6561，比较真实分布、非推理 transformer、带中间 token 的推理 transformer、精确 k-gram 模拟和渐近理论。指标包括叶子自旋和的方差以及峰度。结果显示，有限上下文非推理模型生成的方差随上下文深度近似按 log-linear 规律变化，峰度趋向 Gaussian，而真实语言在长程相关上保持不同统计；只有当上下文覆盖完整树结构时，非推理生成才接近真实分布。带推理 token 的模型明显更接近真实层级统计，说明在这个构造中，中间步骤的收益不是来自更强模型容量，而是来自把隐层结构显式化。理论部分给出大树高、上下文深度与树高存在间隔时的渐近预测，并用 transformer 训练实验验证趋势。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 30 depth 变化。Section 4 和 Figure 1 用来判断理论是否真的解释了训练出的 transformer。推理相关部分重点看额外中间 token 怎样改变条件依赖结构，而不是只看最终指标。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a basic question behind context scaling: what does a longer autoregressive context actually recover, and when can explicit reasoning tokens substitute for that context. Instead of working with natural language directly, it defines a tractable family of hierarchical probabilistic languages generated by broadcast processes on trees, so that long-range dependence, hidden structure, context truncation, and generation errors can be analyzed exactly.","importance":"The main value is conceptual and theoretical. It turns context length and reasoning into measurable quantities under a known data distribution. A finite-context next-token model can be shown to distort global statistics of the generated sequence, even if local prediction is optimal. Reasoning tokens help when they expose latent hierarchical variables and convert long-range dependencies into local conditional information. This is useful for thinking about long-context pretraining and chain-of-thought, but it should not be read as a direct recipe for training production LLMs.","technical_approach":"The central device is an exact k-gram ansatz for a transformer with context length k. The model is replaced by the optimal autoregressive process that conditions only on the previous k tokens. This would be intractable for general language, but it is analyzable for the proposed hierarchical languages. A sequence is produced by a d-ary tree broadcast process: the root token is sampled from a prior, each child is sampled from a transition kernel conditioned on its parent, and the leaves form the observed sequence. The paper studies an Ising broadcast process over ±1 tokens for soft global correlations, and a coloring broadcast process for hard constraints. In experiments, hierarchical punctuation marks are added so bounded-context models can identify their position in the tree. The reasoning variant generates additional intermediate tokens, which effectively reveal part of the hidden tree and reduce the need for a long raw context window.","experiments":"The key empirical check is whether the k-gram theory predicts trained transformers. In the Ising experiment shown in Figure 1, the setting is d=3, h=8, and rho=0.9, giving 3^8=6561 leaves. The paper compares the true language, non-reasoning transformers, reasoning transformers with intermediate tokens, exact autoregressive simulations from the theory, and asymptotic predictions. The statistics are the variance and kurtosis of the sum of leaf spins. The non-reasoning model approaches the true distribution only when the context covers the relevant tree structure; otherwise its variance follows the predicted log-linear behavior in context depth and its kurtosis drifts toward a Gaussian limit. The reasoning model better preserves the hierarchical statistics because it makes latent internal variables available during generation. The transformer curves track the exact k-gram and asymptotic predictions closely enough to support the ansatz in this controlled setting.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 30 to 45 minutes. Read Section 2 first for the broadcast-language construction; the important point is that this is a probability distribution over sequences, not a formal-language membership task. Then read the k-gram ansatz and Theorem 2.2, since this is the bridge from finite-context transformers to exact analysis. Section 3 contains the main scaling predictions for the Ising case. Figure 1 and the surrounding experimental section are the quickest way to judge whether the theory matches trained transformers. For the reasoning claim, focus on how intermediate tokens alter the conditional dependency structure.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看模型定义和两条渐近结论即可；它给 pretrain 的价值在于把“长上下文不等于层级推理”讲干净了。","en":"Read the model definition and the two asymptotic results; the useful prior update is that longer context and hierarchical reasoning are cleanly separated here."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Duke University","University of Chicago","Massachusetts Institute of Technology","Princeton University"],"authors":["Jason Gaitonde","Frederic Koehler","Elchanan Mossel","Joonhyung Shin","Allan Sly"],"notable_authors":["Elchanan Mossel","Allan Sly"],"tags":["scaling-laws","hierarchical-language","reasoning","autoregressive-modeling","theory"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13687","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.13687","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.12874","title":"Descriptive Collision in Sparse Autoencoder Auto-Interpretability: When One Explanation Describes Many Features","abstract":"Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are now standard tools for decomposing language model activations into interpretable features, and automated interpretability pipelines routinely assign each feature a short natural-language explanation. Existing critiques of this practice focus on polysemanticity -- one feature with many meanings -- or on whether explanations predict activations. We identify a complementary, structurally distinct problem we call descriptive collision: many distinct SAE features admit ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-13T01:41:38+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_12874.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的不是 SAE 特征本身是否可解释，而是“解释是否能唯一指向特征”。作者指出，现有 auto-interpretability 评估默认把问题设成：给定一个特征，解释是否准确描述它的激活模式；但即便解释是对的，也可能同时适用于很多别的特征。作者把这种 many-features-to-one-explanation 的现象定义为 descriptive collision，并把它和 polysemanticity 区分开：polysemanticity 是一个特征对应多个语义，collision 是多个特征共享同一个语义描述。前者损害解释完整性，后者损害解释辨识性。论文要解决的核心问题，就是现有基于自然语言解释的 SAE 自动解释与打分体系，是否真的在“识别 feature”，还是只是在给 feature 所在的粗语义簇贴标签。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接意义不在于改进 loss 或架构，而在于给 mechanistic interpretability 里一条常用工作流做了方法学拆解。SAE 已经成为分析 LLM 内部表征的标准工具之一，很多后续结论都建立在“feature 有自然语言标签，且标签可被自动评分验证”这个前提上。作者指出，这个前提缺了一块：高 detection score 只能说明解释抓住了某种可预测模式，不能说明它区分了该 feature 与其他相近 feature。这个问题一旦成立，会影响 feature-level attribution、circuit tracing、feature intervention 结果的可信度边界。贡献量级更像“评估协议层面的校正”，不是提出新 SAE 训练法，但它触及的是解释链条里很基础的一环，因此对依赖 SAE 做 LLM 内部分析的人有现实影响。","technical_approach":"方法上分三层。第一层是定义。作者把 explanation function 形式化为 E: F→Σ*，并定义 collision cluster，即所有共享同一解释字符串的 feature 集合。进一步用 mutual information I(F;A) 衡量 explanation 对 feature 身份的区分能力，称为 descriptive resolution。这个定义很干净，因为它把“解释是否提供了足够多的信息”从语义直觉变成了可计算的信息量问题：如果标签空间远小于 feature 空间，残余歧义是结构性存在的，而不是某个解释器写得不够好。第二层是经验重分析。作者没有重新训练 SAE，也没有调用 LLM 重新生成解释，而是拿 Marks 等人公开的人类标注语料做分析。这样做的好处是噪声更低，能先证明 collision 在高质量人工标签下都已经很严重，那么在更模板化、更保守的 LLM 自动解释里只会更严重。第三层是评估批判与修正。作者证明 detection-style scoring 在 collision-equivalent features 之间具有不变性：如果两个 feature 共享同一解释，基于“解释能否预测 feature 何时激活”的检测式分数，无法证明这个解释识别的是当前 feature 而不是同簇别的 feature。为此作者提出两个修正方向：一是 discrimination scoring，要求解释不仅能预测目标 feature 的激活，还要能把它和碰撞簇中的其他 feature 区分开；二是 collision-adjusted detection，在原有检测分数上引入对碰撞的校正。文中还给了伪代码，并讨论了计算代价。整体上，这不是一个新模型，而是对 auto-interp 评价目标的重新定义：从“描述得对不对”推进到“描述得是否足以识别”。","experiments":"实验部分以公开人工标注集的统计重分析为主，不是大规模新训练。数据来自 Sparse Feature Circuits 相关公开语料，去重后共 722 个标注特征，覆盖 Gemma 2 2B SAE 的 470 个特征和 Pythia 70M SAE 的 277 个特征。核心数字很明确：722 个特征只对应 235 个不同的 annotation string，平均每个标注字符串被复用 3.07 次；82.1% 的特征至少与另一个特征共享同一标注；最常见的字符串“plural nouns”对应 101 个不同特征，跨越 Pythia 70M 与 Gemma 2 2B、18 个层位和 4 类模型组件。这个结果说明 collision 不是局部偶发现象，而是跨模型、跨层、跨组件都存在。论文的理论部分还给出一个命题：对 detection-style 评分，只要 feature 处于同一 collision cluster，替换成簇内其他 feature 不改变该类评分的判别逻辑，因此高分不能作为 feature identification 的证据。作者没有做新的 intervention 实验，也没有比较不同 LLM explainer 的 collision 率，这一点是边界，也是后续工作空间。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和 Definitions，抓住作者到底在批评什么：不是解释错，而是解释不够区分。然后看 empirical evidence 的表格和几个最常见 collision case，理解问题规模。接着看理论命题，重点不是证明技巧，而是 detection score 为什么天然忽略 feature identity。最后看作者提出的 discrimination scoring 和 collision-adjusted detection，理解它们各自补哪一块。预计 25-35 分钟能读完主线。若你平时用 SAE 标签做 feature clustering、circuit tracing 或自动筛选 feature，值得多花一点时间想一个问题：你当前 pipeline 里，解释是被当作“语义摘要”还是“feature 标识符”；这篇文章指出这两者不能混用。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a narrower but more damaging question than standard SAE auto-interpretability critiques: even if a natural-language explanation is accurate, does it uniquely identify the feature? The authors argue that current pipelines mostly evaluate explanation adequacy for a fixed feature, but ignore a dual failure mode where many distinct SAE features admit the same explanation. They call this descriptive collision. It is different from polysemanticity: polysemanticity is one feature mapping to multiple meanings, while collision is multiple features mapping to one description. The core problem is therefore not whether explanations are truthful, but whether explanation-based evaluation can certify feature identity rather than merely assign a coarse semantic label to a cluster of features.","importance":"Its value is methodological rather than architectural, but the target is central. SAE-based feature decomposition is now a common tool for interpreting LLM representations, and many downstream claims rely on the assumption that feature labels produced by humans or LLMs are meaningful and can be validated by detection-style scores. This paper shows that such scores may validate semantic overlap without validating identification. That matters for feature attribution, circuit analysis, intervention studies, and any workflow that treats a short explanation as if it were a stable handle on a specific latent. The contribution is not a new SAE training recipe; it is a correction to what current auto-interpretability metrics can and cannot certify.","technical_approach":"$37","experiments":"$38","reading_guide":"For a skim, start with the introduction and the formal definitions. The key is to understand that the critique is not “the explanation is wrong,” but “the explanation is too non-specific to identify the feature.” Then read the empirical table and the examples of repeated labels to get a sense of scale. After that, look at the proposition about detection-style score invariance; the proof itself is simple, but the implication is the main point. Finally, read the sections on discrimination scoring and collision-adjusted detection to see what a better evaluation target would look like. About 25 to 35 minutes is enough for the main thread. If you use SAE labels for feature search, clustering, or circuit tracing, it is worth pausing on one practical question: are your labels acting as semantic summaries, or as identifiers? The paper argues that current pipelines often conflate the two.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"揭示了 SAE 自动解释中的 'descriptive collision' 现象（多个 feature 对应同一个解释），对做 mech interp 和特征级控制的研究员是很好的避坑指南，建议看量化分析部分。","en":"Reveals the 'descriptive collision' phenomenon in SAE automated interpretation (multiple features mapping to the same explanation), serving as a great pitfall guide for mech interp and feature-level control researchers. whether LLMs use a shared internal representation for preferences across personas, or whether each persona implements its own choice mechanism. Preferences are operationalized as revealed pairwise task choices rather than verbal self-reports.","importance":"The main value is mechanistic rather than pretraining-methodological. The results suggest that post-trained models can encode preference-like quantities in linear residual-stream directions, and that the meaning of such directions is conditioned by the active persona. This matters for white-box safety probes, RLHF-induced representations, and persona-dependent behavior. It also gives a concrete way to study how post-training signals interact with base-model representations.","technical_approach":"The authors fit a utility function from revealed pairwise choices, then train linear probes on residual-stream activations to predict those utilities. The resulting direction is treated as a preference vector. They test it in three ways. First, they check generalization to unseen topics and out-of-distribution preference types, including truth versus falsehood and targeted preference shifts. Second, they perform causal steering on task tokens in Gemma-3-27B to see whether moving along the direction changes pairwise choices. Third, they train on the Assistant persona and evaluate transfer to other personas, including an evil persona whose preferences are anti-correlated with the Assistant. This differs from standard harmfulness or truthfulness probes because the supervision is derived from the model’s own choices.","experiments":"The experiments use Gemma-3-27B and Qwen-3.5-122B. The preference vector predicts revealed choices across prompts and situations, generalizes to unseen topics, and tracks targeted shifts in preference. In Gemma-3-27B, steering along the vector on task tokens has a large causal effect on which task the model completes. In the evil-persona setting, the same vector scores benign tasks higher under Assistant rollouts but reverses under evil-persona rollouts; a text-encoder baseline does not show this inversion. Cross-persona transfer works better than simply predicting Assistant preferences, but it does not explain all variance. ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-13T16:48:48+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_13772.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文处理的是多步推理中的首个错误定位问题。已有方法多给整条推理轨迹一个置信分数，或者依赖多次采样、自一致性、外部 in multi-step reasoning. Most existing detectors assign one confidence score to the whole trace, require multiple sampled completions, or depend on a separate verifier. GeoReason asks whether a single forward pass contains enough hidden-state signal to identify where the reasoning trajectory first breaks.","importance":"Its main value for pretraining research is diagnostic rather than algorithmic. The paper treats reasoning failure as a local anomaly in hidden-state transitions, not merely as high token entropy or an incorrect final answer. This is useful for studying chain-of-thought dynamics, process supervision, and the internal separability of correct versus corrupted reasoning states. The transfer gap is also informative: the label-conditioned teacher transfers more reliably across models and datasets, while the distilled student is fragile under shift. That suggests the signal exists, but preserving it in a deployable detector is the hard part.","technical_approach":"Each reasoning step is represented by pooling token hidden states from a fixed transformer layer. The teacher uses step-level labels to build a trace-specific local coordinate system around the correct prefix, then applies a contrastive PCA lens that emphasizes directions separating coherent transitions from first-error transitions. It scores each step with geometric transition features related to transport instability. The student is a BiLSTM trained to reproduce the teacher’s scores from raw hidden-state sequences, so inference requires no labels, no sampling, and no external verifier. The theory argues that contrastive PCA is optimal for a transport-separation objective, that first-error localization is possible under a transport-margin condition, and that teacher-student agreement depends on preserving that margin.","experiments":"The experiments use ProcessBench, PRM800K, HaluEval, and TruthfulQA, with entropy-based, probing-based, and attention-based baselines. We propose Layer-wise Representation Dynamics (LRD), a framework with three layer-wise measurement families: Frenet (Grassmann speed and curvature) for global subspace motion, Neighborhood Retention Score (NRS) for local nearest-neighbor retention, and Graph Filtration Mutual Information (GFMI) for alignment with the final layer. Applying LRD to 31 mode","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-12T20:22:45+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_12714.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是一个分析层面的问题：现代 embedders 和 base LLM 的表示不是只在最后一层才有差异，层间表示形成过程本身就不同，但现有分析往往只看单一指标，难以同时描述全局子空间变化、局部邻域保持和与最终层的对齐关系。作者提出 LRD，希望用一组互补的 layer-wise 指标，把“表示是如何逐层成形的”描述得更完整，并进一步看看这些动态特征能否用于无标签模型选择和推理时层裁剪。","importance":"对预训练研究的价值主要是诊断和表征分析，而不是直接提出新的训练方法。它的意义在于提供了一套较系统的 layer-wise measurement family，帮助研究者比较不同架构、不同任务上表示形成的路径差异。对于做基座模型的人，这类工具有两个实际用途。第一，它能帮助判断中间层是否已经形成稳定语义结构，从而为 layer selection、early exit、蒸馏或中间层监督提供依据。第二，它提醒人们不要只盯 final layer embedding；很多模型差异发生在形成过程，而不是终点。论文还把这些指标用于 label-free model selection 和 pruning，说明这些分析量不只是可视化工具，而是有一定决策价值。不过它更偏 representation analysis，对预训练主线的影响属于中等。","technical_approach":"LRD 由三类指标组成，分别对应三种不同的表示动态。\n\n第一类是 Frenet family，用来刻画全局子空间运动。作者把每层表示矩阵做中心化后取主奇异子空间，把各层看成 Grassmann 流形上的离散路径。相邻层之间的 Grassmann geodesic distance 定义为 speed，连续三层构成的 Menger curvature 则描述路径是否发生明显转向。这个视角关心的是“全局主方向是否还在变”。\n\n第二类是 NRS，Neighborhood Retention Score，关注局部结构是否被保留。它比较相邻层中同一 anchor 的近邻集合重叠程度。即便全局子空间变化不大，局部邻域也可能重排；反过来也成立。因此 NRS 补上了 Frenet family 看不到的局部几何。\n\n第三类是 GFMI，Graph Filtration Mutual Information，用来衡量某一中间层与最终层组织结构的对齐程度。作者不是直接做简单相似度，而是通过图过滤后的互信息来比较层间结构，这样更强调拓扑/关系层面的接近程度。它回答的是“这一层已经多像最终层了”。\n\n方法上的一个细节是，作者对每个 model-task pair 固定一个子空间维度 r，取能解释 final layer 95% 方差的最小秩，并把这个 r 用到所有层上。这样做的好处是层间可比，但也隐含了 final layer 作为参照的偏置。整体上，三类指标分别对应 global motion、local retention、final-layer alignment，互补性是这篇工作的核心。","experiments":"实验覆盖 31 个现成模型和 30 个 MTEB 任务，其中包括 25 个 embedders（encoder-based 和 decoder-based）以及 6 个 base LLM；任务类型包括 classification、retrieval、STS、reranking 和 duplicate detection。这个覆盖面比较大，足以支持作者关于“架构差异”和“任务差异”的经验结论。\n\n主要结果有三层。第一，LRD 确实揭示出 final-layer 表示看不出来的差异。不同架构在层间 speed、curvature、neighbor retention 和 final-layer alignment 上呈现出不同轨迹；不同任务类型也会改变这些轨迹形状。这说明“表示形成过程”本身是有结构的，而不是单调趋近最终层。\n\n第二，在无标签模型选择上，作者把 layer-wise 指标汇总成 model-level score，发现三类分数都与下游性能正相关，其中 d_{0,L} 最强。这里的含义是：从输入层到最终层的整体表征位移，可能是一个相对稳健的无监督质量信号。虽然摘要里没有给出完整相关系数，但全文结论是这些分数确实能在不看标签的情况下帮助挑模型。\n\n第三，在 layer pruning 上，GFMI 优于随机裁剪，在 15% 和 20% 的预算下表现更好，并且在所有预算上都有最佳 median change。也就是说，如果必须删层，优先保留那些对最终层结构对齐更关键的层，比随机删更稳。\n\n从预训练研究者角度，最有用的实验不是某个具体数值，而是作者证明了 layer-wise dynamics 可以转化成可操作的选择信号，而不只是分析图。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先看方法定义部分，把三类指标各自测什么弄清楚。尤其要分清：Frenet family 看的是主子空间路径，NRS 看的是局部近邻保持，GFMI 看的是与最终层的结构对齐。很多读者会把它们都当成“层间相似度”，这样会错过论文真正的信息。\n\n然后看结果部分里按模型类型和任务类型展开的图。这里最值得关注的不是哪条曲线高，而是曲线形状：哪些模型前层变化快、后层趋稳，哪些模型中层发生明显转向，哪些任务导致局部邻域在后层才稳定。最后再看两个应用实验：label-free model selection 和 pruning。它们决定这套分析是否有工程价值。\n\n如果时间有限，40 分钟左右可以完成一次有效泛读。若要深挖，建议重点检查 GFMI 的定义和实现，因为它是最不直观但也最可能带来实际 pruning 信号的部分；另外也要留意作者如何把 layer-wise 曲线压缩成 model-level score，这关系到方法能否迁移到别的数据和模型族。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"This paper studies a representation-analysis problem rather than a new training method. Modern embedders and base LLMs do not only differ in their final-layer states; they also differ in how representations are formed across layers. Existing analyses often focus on a single criterion, making it hard to jointly characterize global subspace motion, local neighborhood preservation, and alignment with the final layer. LRD is proposed as a unified layer-wise framework to describe these dynamics more completely, and to test whether such signals are useful for label-free model selection and inference-time layer pruning.","importance":"For pretraining researchers, the value is diagnostic. LRD offers a structured way to compare how different architectures and tasks shape representations through depth, rather than judging models only by the final embedding. That matters for several practical reasons: identifying semantically mature intermediate layers, informing early-exit or pruning decisions, guiding distillation targets, and understanding whether architectural changes alter the trajectory of representation formation rather than just the endpoint. The paper also shows that these measurements can support model selection and pruning, so they are not merely descriptive plots. Still, the contribution is analytical rather than a direct advance in pretraining methodology.","technical_approach":"$39","experiments":"$3a","reading_guide":"$3b","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看指标定义和模型选择相关性即可；LRD 像一套可复用诊断仪，剪层结论别过度外推到生成式 pretrain。","en":"Read the metric definitions and selection correlations; LRD is a reusable diagnostic, while the pruning result should not be overextended to generative pretraining."},"metadata":{"institutions":["The Hong Kong University of Science and Technology"],"authors":["Jingzhou Jiang","Yi Yang","Kar Yan Tam"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["representation-analysis","layer-dynamics","MTEB","model-selection","pruning"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12714","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.12714","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.12922","title":"When Attention Closes: How LLMs Lose the Thread in Multi-Turn Interaction","abstract":"Large language models can follow complex instructions in a single turn, yet over long multi-turn interactions they often lose the thread of instructions, persona, and rules. This degradation has been measured behaviorally but not mechanistically explained. We propose a channel-transition account: goal-defining tokens become less accessible through attention, while goal-related information may persist in residual representations. We introduce the Goal Accessibility Ratio (GAR), measuring attentio","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-13T02:58:18+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_12922.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"解释多轮对话中模型为何会逐步“丢掉系统提示里的目标/规则/人设”，并给出可预测、可诊断的机制指标。论文提出一个两通道解释：目标信息一开始主要通过注意力通道从系统提示 的注意力质量（跨层跨头平均）。它不是通用质量分数，而是架构相关的诊断量。\n2）Sliding-window ablation：用滑动窗口裁剪上下文，因果地“关闭”对系统提示的直接注意力通道，并观察行为何时崩。\n3）Residual-stream probes：在线性可分性层面探测残差流中是否仍可解码出“本回合是否能回忆/遵循目标”的结果变量，用 AUC 衡量；并用输入 embedding gradually lose adherence to system-prompt goals (instructions/persona/rules) over long multi-turn interactions, and proposes a mechanistic, predictive diagnostic. It argues for a two-channel transition: early on, goal tokens are directly accessible via attention; as turns progress, attention to those goal-defining tokens decays and eventually enters a “closure” regime, after which behavior depends on whether goal state has been preserved indirectly in residual-stream representations.","importance":"Not a pretraining-method paper, but it is useful for architecture/optimization-facing researchers because it turns a messy behavioral failure into measurable internal quantities. GAR can serve as a regression metric for long-dialogue instruction-following. The finding that goal information becomes linearly decodable at very different depths across architectures (layer ~2 to ~27) suggests that “what survives after attention closure” is an architectural property—relevant when designing mid-training or alignment interventions for long-context and multi-turn settings.","technical_approach":"The proposed channel-transition account decomposes goal-conditioned state propagation into:\n- An attention channel: direct attention mass from generated tokens to goal-defining tokens in the system prompt.\n- A residual channel: goal-related information written into and carried by the residual stream.\n\nThree instruments:\n1) Goal Accessibility Ratio (GAR): average attention mass from generated tokens to the goal-token set, aggregated across heads/layers.\n2) Sliding-window ablations: causally close the attention channel by truncating context windows and observing when compliance collapses.\n3) Linear residual-stream probes: test whether per-episode compliance/recall outcomes are decodable from residual activations (AUC), with input-embedding probes as a control.\n\nA key claim is that the turn at which attention “closes” is parametrically predictable from window size (a near-perfect linear dose-response).","experiments":"Across four open-weight architectures (Mistral, Qwen, LLaMA, Mixtral):\n- GAR declines monotonically with turn count for every architecture (Mann–Kendall p < 1e-7).\n- Sliding-window ablations show a linear dose-response between window size and the crossover/closure turn (R^2 > 0.999), supporting a strong causal link.\n- Residual-stream probes predict per-episode recall/compliance with AUC up to 0.99, while input-embedding probes are near chance—evidence that goal state persists in residual representations even when attention access fades.\n- The layer at which goal information becomes linearly decodable varies widely by architecture (as early as layer 2, as late as layer 27).\n- Post-closure behavioral differences: Mistral degrades more gracefully with goal complexity; LLaMA/Qwen fail more uniformly; Mixtral shows a sharp mid-depth “phase-transition-like” rise in decodability.\n\nOverall, the contribution is a reproducible diagnostic framework rather than a performance benchmark.","reading_guide":"Skim plan (~30–45 minutes):\n1) Start with the two-channel large language model pretraining, with the appealing interpretation that it provides a content-addressed shortcut to recurring local token patterns. We ask whether this interpretation transfers to autoregressive (AR) image generation, or whether the observed gains, if any, come from a different mechanism. We adapt the Engram module to vision with 2D spatial $n$-gram hashin","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-13T08:40:46+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_13179.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文检验 Engram 这类 hash-keyed O(1) 记忆模块在自回归图像生成中是否真的做了内容寻址式检索。问题不只是“加模块是否提升 FID”，而是要区分三种可能机制：表项内容被正确检索、gated side-pathway 带来额外表达能力、或者模块只起到正则化作用。","importance":"对预训练研究的价值在于给外部记忆模块提供了一个清晰的负结果和诊断范式。Engram 在语言模型中常被解释为对重复局部 token 模式的快速检索；如果这个解释成立，离散图像 token 的局部冗余应当更适合它。但论文发现，在 ImageNet 256×256 的 AR 图像生成上，Engram 没有在参数匹配条件下优于纯 Transformer。更关键的是，模型对 memory table 是否多样、是否被训练敏感，但对 hash key 是否语义匹配并不敏感。这提醒预训练研究中很多“记忆”模块的收益可能来自额外残差路径和门控混合，而不是预期的内容检索。","technical_approach":"作者将 NLP 中的 Engram 改造成适合二维离散图像 token 的版本。图像先经 VQ 类编码器变成 16×16 token grid，再按 raster scan 做自回归建模。Engram 使用局部 n-gram 的 hash 作为地址，从大表中 O(1) 取出向量，并通过 gate 与 Transformer hidden state 融合。视觉版本加入了二维空间 n-gram hashing，包括 1×3、3×1、2×2 等 causal 局部模式；同时使用 gated fusion、per-channel LayerScale，并保证增量采样时与 KV-cache 兼容。为了判断模块是否真的检索内容，作者设计了三类诊断：推理时固定 gate 的 gate-clamp analysis；替换 hash 输入来源的 donor probe；从头训练时把 memory table 冻结为高斯噪声的控制实验。","experiments":"实验在 class-conditional ImageNet 256×256 AR 生成上做参数预算扫描，backbone-to-total parameter ratio ρ 覆盖 0.17 到 0.90。纯 AR baseline 的 FID 为 2.02，所有 Engram 变体在参数匹配下都更差。加入更丰富的二维 n-gram bank 后，FID 变化小于 0.01，说明局部空间 hash 形式不是主要瓶颈。gate-clamp 实验显示，将 learned gate 替换为常数 g=0.10 或 0.25 可以匹配或超过 learned gate；g=1 会使 FID-10K 从 4.89 变差到 6.53；g=0 则导致生成崩坏，FID-10K 达到 115。这说明 side-pathway 需要存在，但输出不能被过度混入。donor probe 中，用 matched、adversarial 或 random same-class 样本提供 hash token，next-token 分布几乎无法区分；但如果折叠 bucket index 或把表替换为随机噪声，分布会恶化两到三个数量级。最后，从头训练且把整张 memory table 冻结为 N(0,1) 噪声，只比学习表差 ΔFID=0.10，Inception Score 反而从 259 提到 285。整体结果支持一个判断：Engram 在该设置下主要是 gated architectural side-pathway，learned table 内容只提供小幅修正。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 Introduction 和 Method，重点看作者如何把 1D Engram 改造成二维 causal hashing，并保持 KV-cache 兼容。实验部分优先看三组诊断：gate clamp、donor probe、frozen-noise table。预计阅读时间 25–35 分钟。最值得带走的是诊断设计，而不是图像生成指标本身。类似方法可用于检查 LLM 中的外部记忆、检索增强层、稀疏表参数是否真的按内容寻址工作，还是只提供额外路径和噪声正则。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tests whether the Engram module, a hash-keyed O(1) associative memory, actually performs content-addressed retrieval in autoregressive image generation. The key question is not only whether FID improves, but whether any gain comes from retrieved memory contents, from the gated side pathway, or from regularization-like effects.","importance":"The paper is useful for pretraining research because it gives a careful negative result on external memory modules. Engram is often described as a shortcut to recurring local token patterns. If that interpretation were correct, discrete image tokens should be a favorable setting because local visual patches are highly redundant. Instead, on ImageNet 256×256 AR generation, Engram does not outperform a pure Transformer at matched parameter count. More importantly, the model cares that the table is diverse and present, but not that the queried entry semantically matches the current context. This is a useful warning for memory-augmented pretraining: measured gains may come from an extra gated residual stream rather than real content retrieval.","technical_approach":"The authors adapt Engram from 1D language sequences to 2D VQ image-token grids. Images are encoded into a 16×16 grid of discrete tokens and modeled in raster order by a causal Transformer. Engram hashes recent local n-grams into a large table, retrieves a vector in O(1), and fuses it into the hidden state through a learned gate. The vision version adds spatial n-gram hashing over causal 2D patterns such as 1×3, 3×1, and 2×2, uses gated fusion with per-channel LayerScale, and supports incremental decoding with KV-cache. The diagnostic toolkit is the main contribution: gate-clamp analysis to control mixing strength, donor probes that replace hash inputs with matched/adversarial/random examples, and from-scratch training with the entire memory table frozen to Gaussian noise.","experiments":"$3c","reading_guide":"Skim the Introduction and Method to understand the 2D adaptation of Engram and the KV-cache-compatible implementation.
